I have developed effective ways to help leaders coax and promote change, resulting in improved organizational performance.  \n  \nIn coaching sessions\u2014one-to-one or in group/team settings\u2014I create a space with care, where people feel completely open to discuss important issues or confront a challenging topic. What I mean by \u201cmarket size:\u201d I define it as annual revenue generated by coach practitioners (not company valuations). This follows the ICF Global Coaching Study, the most widely recognized benchmark in the industry. Primary sources: ICF\u2019s latest studies reveal total revenue, coach headcount, fees, and regional figures. CAGR (compound annual growth rate) and 2030 projections are based on ICF reports between 2020\u20132025. And to fill in gaps, I reference reputable niche/adjacent reports. What I exclude: I don\u2019t include software/platform valuations or training/LMS markets in the overall coaching market size figure. Including those is the main reason you\u2019ll see inflated estimates in a lot of other reports.\n\nOne challenge with coaching market data is that different reports define \u201ccoaching\u201d differently. COACHING SIMPLIFICATION SYSTEM\n\n### Problem\nExecutive coaches spend 30\u201340% of their time on admin: organizing files, building presentations, writing follow-ups, scheduling, and manually tracking progress. Meredith's practice spans 9 service offerings across multiple delivery formats. Without centralization, this creates:\n- Scattered documents across email, Dropbox, Google Drive, local files\n- Manual session notes with no searchability\n- No standardized onboarding process\n- Presentation creation from scratch each time\n- No automated follow-up after sessions\n- No visibility into which clients need attention\n\n### Solution: BoldEdge Coaching OS\n\n#### Module 1: File Organization System\n```\nBoldEdge File Structure (per client)\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 /intake\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 diagnostic-results.pdf\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 intake-questionnaire.json\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 signed-agreement.pdf\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 /sessions\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 2026-01-15-session-notes.md\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 2026-02-01-session-notes.md\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 action-items-tracker.json\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 /assessments\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 disc-profile.pdf\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 5-behaviors-report.pdf\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 360-feedback-summary.pdf\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 /documents\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 org-chart.pdf\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 strategic-plan.docx\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 financial-summary.xlsx\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 /presentations\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 quarterly-review-Q1.pptx\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 board-presentation.pptx\n\u2514\u2500\u2500 /reports\n    \u251c\u2500\u2500 progress-report-month-3.pdf\n    \u2514\u2500\u2500 roi-summary-annual.pdf\n```\n\n**Auto-Classification:** Upload any file \u2192 AI classifies into correct folder\n**Version Control:** All documents versioned, never lost\n**Search:** Full-text search across all client documents\n**Tagging:** Auto-tag by topic (finance, leadership, culture, strategy, people)\n\n#### Module 2: Template Library\n| Template Category | Templates Included | Auto-Populated Fields |\n|------------------|-------------------|----------------------|\n| **Intake & Diagnostic** | Business Diagnostic Questionnaire, Coaching Agreement, Goals Worksheet, Team Assessment | Client name, date, company info |\n| **Session Management** | Session Agenda, Session Notes, Action Item Tracker, Follow-Up Email | Date, client name, previous action items |\n| **Assessments** | DiSC Prep Guide, 5 Behaviors Framework, 360 Feedback Template, Leadership Style Assessment | Participant name, role, assessment date |\n| **Presentations** | Quarterly Progress Review, Annual Business Diagnostic, Board Presentation, ROI Summary | KPI data, progress charts, goals |\n| **Reports** | Monthly Progress Report, Quarterly Business Review, Annual Impact Summary, Client NPS Report | Pulled from dashboard analytics |\n| **Programs** | RISE Enrollment Kit, CEO Roundtable Welcome Pack, Peer Group Onboarding, Retreat Pre-Work | Participant info, schedule, materials |\n| **SOPs** | New Client Onboarding SOP, Session Preparation SOP, Follow-Up SOP, Billing SOP, Referral SOP | Role assignments, timelines |\n\n#### Module 3: Presentation Builder\n- **Pre-Built Slides:** 50+ coaching-specific slide templates\n- **Data Integration:** Charts auto-populate from client dashboard data\n- **Brand Consistency:** Meredith's branding auto-applied (colors, fonts, logo)\n- **Export Formats:** PowerPoint, PDF, Google Slides\n- **Quick Builds:** \"Generate Quarterly Review\" \u2192 pulls 3 months of data \u2192 creates 12-slide deck in 30 seconds\n\n#### Module 4: Onboarding Automation\n```\nTrigger: New client added to BoldEdge\n\u2192 Day 0: Welcome email sent with login credentials\n\u2192 Day 0: Diagnostic questionnaire link sent\n\u2192 Day 1: Reminder if questionnaire not started\n\u2192 Day 2: Coaching agreement sent for e-signature\n\u2192 Day 3: Reminder if agreement not signed\n\u2192 Day 3: Pre-session prep guide sent\n\u2192 Day 5: Calendar invite for first session (auto-scheduled)\n\u2192 Day 7: First session \u2192 diagnostic results auto-populated in dashboard\n\u2192 Day 8: Follow-up email with action items\n\u2192 Day 14: Check-in email\n\u2192 Day 30: First progress report auto-generated\n```\n\n#### Module 5: SOP Dashboard\n| SOP | Trigger | Steps | Auto-Actions |\n|-----|---------|-------|-------------|\n| New Client Onboarding | Client added | 8 steps, 14 days | Welcome email, questionnaire, agreement, calendar invite |\n| Session Preparation | 24 hours before session | 4 steps | Pull client dashboard, review action items, prep agenda |\n| Post-Session Follow-Up | Session marked complete | 3 steps | Email action items, update dashboard, schedule next session |\n| Monthly Check-In | 1st of each month | 2 steps | Generate progress report, send to client |\n| Quarterly Review | End of quarter | 5 steps | Generate QBR deck, schedule review meeting, send prep materials |\n| Annual Diagnostic | Client anniversary | 4 steps | Re-run diagnostic, compare year-over-year, generate annual report |\n| Client At-Risk | No session in 30 days | 3 steps | Alert coach, send re-engagement email, offer check-in |\n| NPS Survey | 90 days post-start | 2 steps | Send NPS survey, trigger referral request if score 9-10 |\n| Billing Reminder | 5 days before invoice | 1 step | Auto-invoice via Stripe, receipt email |\n\n#### Module 6: Coach Dashboard (Meredith's View)\n**Daily View:**\n- Today's sessions (with prep materials linked)\n- Overdue action items across all clients\n- New documents uploaded by clients\n- Upcoming deadlines\n\n**Weekly View:**\n- Session count and utilization rate\n- Client progress summary (green/yellow/red)\n- Revenue this week/month\n- Tasks completed vs. outstanding\n\n**Monthly View:**\n- Client retention rate\n- Average session frequency per client\n- Revenue trends\n- NPS scores\n- Top coaching themes across all clients\n- New leads in pipeline\n\n---\n\n## IMPLEMENTATION TIMELINE (5 Weeks)\n\n### Week 1: Foundation\n| Day | Task | Owner | Deliverable |\n|-----|------|-------|-------------|\n| 1 | Database schema deployment | Ned | z.string().email(),\n  password: z.string(),\n});\n\n// POST /api/auth/register\nauthRouter.post(\"/register\", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  const { email, password, name, role } = registerSchema.parse(req.body);\n\n  const existing = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email } });\n  if (existing) throw new AppError(409, \"Email already registered\", \"EMAIL_EXISTS\");\n\n  const passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(password, 12);\n  const user = await prisma.user.create({\n    data: {\n      email,\n      passwordHash,\n      name,\n      role: role as any,\n    },\n  });\n\n  // Create subscription (trial)\n  await prisma.subscription.create({\n    data: {\n      userId: user.id,\n      tier: \"STARTER\",\n      status: \"TRIALING\",\n      trialEndsAt: new res.cookie(\"token\", token, { httpOnly: true, secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === \"production\", sameSite: \"lax\", maxAge: 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 });\n  res.status(201).json({ user: { id: user.id, loginSchema.parse(req.body);\n\n  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email } });\n  if (!user || !user.passwordHash) throw new AppError(401, \"Invalid credentials\", \"INVALID_CREDENTIALS\");\n\n  const valid = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.passwordHash);\n  if (!valid) throw new AppError(401, \"Invalid credentials\", \"INVALID_CREDENTIALS\");\n\n  const });\n});\n\n// POST /api/auth/logout\nauthRouter.post(\"/logout\", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  res.clearCookie(\"token\");\n  res.json({ message: \"Logged out\" });\n});\n",

(!acc[q.category]) acc[q.category] = [];\n    acc[q.category].push(q);\n    return acc;\n  }, {} as Record<string, typeof questions>);\n\n  res.json({ questions: grouped });\n});\n\n// POST (req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  const { responses } = submitResponsesSchema.parse(req.body);\n  const diagnosticId = req.params.id;\n\n  const diagnostic = await prisma.diagnostic.findUnique({ responses.map((r) => ({\n      diagnosticId,\n      questionId: r.questionId,\n      value: r.value,\n      numericValue: r.numericValue,\n    })),\n  });\n\n  // Calculate scores per category\n  const questions = await prisma.diagnosticQuestion.findMany({ where: { active: true } });\n  const questionMap = new Map(questions.map((q) => [q.id, q]));\n\n  const categoryScores: Record<string, { total: number; maxPossible: number; count: number }> = {};\n\n  for (const r of responses) {\n    const q = questionMap.get(r.questionId);\n    if (!q) continue;\n    const cat = q.category;\n    if (!categoryScores[cat]) categoryScores[cat] = { total: 0, maxPossible: 0, count: 0 };\n    categoryScores[cat].total += (r.numericValue || 0) * q.weight;\n    categoryScores[cat].maxPossible += 10 * q.weight; // max score per question is 10\n    categoryScores[cat].count++;\n  }\n\n  // Normalize to 0-100 and calculate gaps\n  const scores = Object.entries(categoryScores)\n    .map(([category, data]) => ({\n      score: Math.round((data.total / data.maxPossible) * 100),\n      maxScore: 100,\n      gap: Math.round(100 - (data.total / data.maxPossible) * 100),\n      category: category as DiagnosticCategory,\n    }))\n    .sort((a, b) => b.gap - a.gap)\n    .map((s, i) => ({ ...s, priority: i + 1 }));\n\n  // Save scores\n  await prisma.diagnosticScore.createMany({\n    data: scores.map((s) => ({ diagnosticId, ...s })),\n  });\n\n  // Generate recommendations based on gaps\n  const recommendations = generateRecommendations(scores, diagnostic.clientId);\n  await [\"report\", \"summary\", \"analysis\", \"progress\", \"roi\"],\n  documents: [\"org\", \"chart\", \"plan\", \"strategy\", \"financial\", \"budget\"],\n};\n\nfunction classifyDocument(filename: }\n  return \"general\";\n}\n\ndocumentRouter.get(\"/\", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  const { clientId, category, folder } = req.query;\n  const where: any = {};\n  if (clientId) Math.round(((sessionsThisMonth - sessionsLastMonth) / sessionsLastMonth) * 100)\n    : 0;\n  const taskCompletionRate = tasksTotal > 0 ? Math.round((tasksCompleted / tasksTotal) * 100) : 0;\n\n  res.json({\n    metrics: {\n      totalClients,\n      activeClients,\n      retentionRate: totalClients > 0 ? Math.round((activeClients / totalClients) * 100) : 0,\n      sessionsThisMonth,\n      sessionTrend,\n      taskCompletionRate,\n      diagnosticsCompleted,\n    },\n  });\n});\n\n// GET /api/analytics/client/:clientId \u2014 client-level analytics\nanalyticsRouter.get(\"/client/:clientId\", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  const clientId = req.params.clientId;\n  const [kpis, sessions, tasks, diagnostics] = await Promise.all([\n    prisma.kPI.findMany({ where: { clientId }, orderBy: { recordedAt: \"desc\" }, take: 200 }),\n    prisma.session.findMany({ where: { clientId, status: \"completed\" }, orderBy: { scheduledAt: \"asc\" } }),\n    prisma.task.findMany({ where: { clientId } }),\n    prisma.diagnostic.findMany({ where: { clientId }, include: { scores: true }, orderBy: { createdAt: \"asc\" } }),\n  ]);\n\n  // KPI trending\n  const kpiTrends = kpis.reduce((acc, k) => {\n    if (!acc[k.name]) acc[k.name] = [];\n    acc[k.name].push({ value: k.value, date: k.recordedAt, target: k.target });\n    return acc;\n  }, {} as Record<string, Array<{ value: number; date: Date; target: number | null }>>);\n\n  // Session frequency\n  const sessionFrequency = sessions.length > 1\n    ? Math.round(sessions.length / (Math.max(1, (Date.now() - sessions[0].scheduledAt.getTime()) / (30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000))))\n    : 0;\n\n  // Task metrics\n  const completed = tasks.filter((t) => t.status === \"COMPLETED\").length;\n  const overdue = tasks.filter((t) => t.status === \"OVERDUE\").length;\n\n  // Diagnostic progress over time\n  const diagnosticProgress = diagnostics.map((d) => ({\n    date: d.createdAt,\n    scores: d.scores.reduce((acc, s) => {\n      acc[s.category] = s.score;\n      return acc;\n    }, {} as Record<string, number>),\n  }));\n\n  res.json({\n    kpiTrends,\n    sessionFrequency,\n    totalSessions: sessions.length,\n    taskMetrics: { total: tasks.length, completed, overdue, completionRate: tasks.length > 0 ? Math.round((completed / tasks.length) * 100) : 0 },\n    diagnosticProgress,\n  });\n});\n\n// POST /api/analytics/kpi \u2014 record a KPI data point\nanalyticsRouter.post(\"/kpi\", authorize(\"COACH\", \"ADMIN\"), async (req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  const kpi = await prisma.kPI.create({ data: req.body });\n  res.status(201).json({ kpi });\n});\n\n// POST /api/analytics/kpi/bulk \u2014 record multiple KPI data points\nanalyticsRouter.post(\"/kpi/bulk\", authorize(\"COACH\", \"ADMIN\"), async (req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  const { kpis } = req.body;\n  await prisma.kPI.createMany({ data: kpis });\n  res.status(201).json({ message: `${kpis.length} KPIs recorded` });\n});\n",

\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n// src/api/routes/webhooks.ts \u2014 Stripe webhook handler\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\nimport { Router, Request, Response } from \"express\";\nimport express from \"express\";\nimport Stripe from \"stripe\";\nimport { prisma } from \"../../utils/prisma\";\nimport { config } from \"../../config\";\nimport { logger } from \"../../utils/logger\";\nimport { SubscriptionTier } from \"@prisma/client\";\n\nexport const webhookRouter = Router();\nconst stripe = new Stripe(config.stripe.secretKey);\n\nconst tierLimits: Record<string, { maxClients: number; maxUsers: number }> = {\n  STARTER: { maxClients: 10, maxUsers: 1 },\n  PROFESSIONAL: { maxClients: 30, maxUsers: 2 },\n  BUSINESS: { maxClients: 100, maxUsers: 5 },\n  ENTERPRISE: { maxClients: -1, maxUsers: -1 },\n};\n\nwebhookRouter.post(\"/stripe\", express.raw({ type: \"application/json\" }), async (req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  const sig = req.headers[\"stripe-signature\"] as string;\n  let event: Stripe.Event;\n\n  try {\n    event = stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(req.body, sig, config.stripe.webhookSecret);\n  } catch (err: any) {\n    logger.error(\"Stripe webhook signature verification failed:\", err.message);\n    return res.status(400).send(`Webhook Error: ${err.message}`);\n  }\n\n  switch (event.type) {\n    case \"checkout.session.completed\": {\n      const session = event.data.object as Stripe.Checkout.Session;\n      const { userId, tier, referredByPartnerId } = session.metadata || {};\n      if (!userId) break;\n\n      const subscription = await stripe.subscriptions.retrieve(session.subscription as string);\n      const limits = tierLimits[tier || \"STARTER\"];\n\n      await prisma.subscription.upsert({\n        where: { userId },\n        update: {\n          tier: (tier || \"STARTER\") as SubscriptionTier,\n       "file_text": "import { prisma } from \"../utils/prisma\";\nimport { logger } from \"../utils/logger\";\nimport { sendEmail, emailTemplates } from \"./email\";\nimport { config } from \"../config\";\n\ninterface StepConfig {\n  type: string;\n  emailTemplate?: string;\n  subject?: string;\n  body?: string;\n  taskTitle?: string;\n  taskDescription?: string;\n  taskPriority?: string;\n  notificationType?: string;\n  notificationMessage?: string;\n}\n\nexport async function processWorkflowInstances(): Promise<void> {\n  const instances = await prisma.workflowInstance.findMany({\n    where: { status: \"running\" },\n    include: {\n      workflow: { include: { steps: { orderBy: { sortOrder: \"asc\" } } } },\n      client: { include: { user: true, coach: { include: { user: true } } } },\n    },\n  });\n\n  for (const instance of instances) {\n    const steps = instance.workflow.steps;\n    if (instance.currentStep >= steps.length) {\n      await prisma.workflowInstance.update({\n        where: { id: instance.id },\n        data: { status: \"completed\", completedAt: new Date() },\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const step = steps[instance.currentStep];\n    const stepConfig = step.config as StepConfig;\n\n    // Check delay\n    if (step.delayHours > 0) {\n      const lastLog = await prisma.workflowLog.findFirst({\n        where: { instanceId: instance.id },\n        orderBy: { executedAt: \"desc\" },\n      });\n      const stepStart = lastLog?.executedAt || instance.startedAt;\n      const delayMs = step.delayHours * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n      if (Date.now() - stepStart.getTime() < delayMs) continue;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      await executeStep(instance, step, stepConfig);\n      await prisma.workflowLog.create({\n        data: {\n          instanceId: instance.id,\n          stepIndex: instance.currentStep,\n          action: stepConfig.type,\n          result: \"success\",\n        },\n      });\n      await prisma.workflowInstance.update({\n        where: { id: instance.id },\n        data: { currentStep: instance.currentStep + 1 },\n      });\n    } catch (error: any) {\n      logger.error(`Workflow step failed: instance=${instance.id}, step=${instance.currentStep}`, error);\n      await prisma.workflowLog.create({\n        data: {\n          instanceId: instance.id,\n          stepIndex: instance.currentStep,\n          action: stepConfig.type,\n          error: error.message,\n        },\n      });\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nasync function executeStep(instance: any, _step: any, stepConfig: StepConfig): Promise<void> {\n  const client = instance.client;\n  const clientUser = client?.user;\n  const coachUser = client?.coach?.user;\n\n  switch (stepConfig.type) {\n    case \"send_email\": {\n      if (!clientUser?.email) break;\n      if (stepConfig.emailTemplate && stepConfig.emailTemplate in emailTemplates) {\n        const templateFn = emailTemplates[stepConfig.emailTemplate as keyof typeof emailTemplates] as Function;\n        const loginUrl = `${config.frontendUrl}/dashboard`;\n        const emailData = templateFn(clientUser.name, loginUrl);\n        await sendEmail({ to: clientUser.email, ...emailData });\n      } else if (stepConfig.subject && stepConfig.body) {\n        await sendEmail({ to: clientUser.email, subject: stepConfig.subject, html: stepConfig.body });\n      }\n      break;\n    }\n\n    case \"create_task\": {\n      if (!client || !clientUser) break;\n      await prisma.task.create({\n        data: {\n          clientId: client.id,\n          assigneeId: clientUser.id,\n          creatorId: coachUser?.id || clientUser.id,\n          title: stepConfig.taskTitle || \"Action Required\",\n          description: stepConfig.taskDescription,\n          priority: (stepConfig.taskPriority as any) || \"MEDIUM\",\n        },\n      });\n      break;\n    }\n\n    case \"send_notification\": {\n      if (!clientUser) break;\n      await \\\"asc\\\" } } } },\\n      client: { include: { user: true, coach: { include: { user: true } } } },\\n    },\\n  });\\n\\n  for (const instance of instances) {\\n    const steps = stepConfig.type,\\n          error: error.message,\\n        },\\n      });\\n    }\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nasync function executeStep(instance: any, _step: any, stepConfig: StepConfig): Promise<void> {\\n
